How does MetricPoller.send() work? Code signature: public send(Map contexts, String errorCode, String error) { ... } File: src/main/java/com/mindarray/nms/MetricPoller.java
In `MetricPoller.send()`: Dequeues availability metrics for processing.

This method: 1. Retrieves pending availability metrics from the queue 2. Groups them by IP address to avoid duplicate polling 3. Batches them for efficient processing (up to 200 metrics per worker) 4. Decrements the idle worker count for each batch created

The method handles duplicate IP addresses by: - Keeping only one metric for each unique IP in the batch - Storing duplicates in the duplicateMetrics map for later processing

It also implements a probing mechanism to avoid infinite loops when: - No more metrics are available - No more idle workers are available @return A list of maps, where each map contains IP-to-metric mappings for a batch / private List> dequeue() { var metrics = new ArrayList>(); var probes = 0; var batchSize = 200; //1 worker means 200 availability metrics... try { while (idleAvailabilityWorkers > 0 && probes < 5) // if probe is greater than 5 means metrics not present or worker not available { for (var pendingMetrics : pendingAvailabilityMetrics.values()) { if (idleAvailabilityWorkers > 0) { var iterator = pendingMetrics.iterator(); if (iterator.hasNext()) { var metric = new HashMap(); metrics.add(metric); var count = 0; while (count < batchSize && iterator.hasNext()) { var id = iterator.next(); if (MetricCacheStore.getStore().queuedMetric(id)) { var event = MetricCacheStore.getStore().dequeue(id); if (EventCacheStore.getStore().validItem(event.getLong(EventBusConstants.EVENT_ID))) { count++; EventBusConstants.updateEvent(event.getLong(EventBusConstants.EVENT_ID), String.format(InfoMessageConstants.EVENT_TRACKER_EVENT_QUALIFIED, DateTimeUtil.timestamp())); // if two same ip polls collides because of any reason than we will ping for any one & equivalent result will be sent if (metric.containsKey(event.getString(OBJECT_IP))) { duplicateMetrics.computeIfAbsent(event.getString(OBJECT_IP), value -> new ArrayList<>()); duplicateMetrics.get(event.getString(OBJECT_IP)).add(event); } else { metric.put(event.getString(OBJECT_IP), event); } } } else { abort(id); } iterator.remove(); } idleAvailabilityWorkers--; } else { probes++; } } else { probes++; } } } } catch (Exception exception) { LOGGER.error(exception); } return metrics; } / Sends error information for multiple contexts.

This method iterates through a map of contexts and processes each one with the extract method, which formats the error information and sends it. After processing each context, it removes it from the map to free up memory and prevent duplicate processing.

The method determines the appropriate error message based on the error code: - For PROCESS_TIMEOUT errors: Uses the PROCESS_TIMED_OUT message - For other errors: Uses the INTERNAL_ERROR message @param contexts A map of target IPs to JsonObject contexts to process @param errorCode The error code to set for all contexts @param error The detailed error information (stack trace), can be null
the NMS engine with the specified contexts, error code, and error message. This method formats the contexts into a JSON array and sends it as a "metric.poll" event to the NMS engine. The event includes: - Event type: "metric.poll" - Contexts: Array of metric contexts - Error code: If applicable - Error message: If applicable If the contexts are empty or null, the method returns without sending anything. The method uses the GlobalCache singleton to send the event to the NMS engine, ensuring proper distribution of the event within the system. How does JobScheduler.REDISCOVER() work? Code signature: public REDISCOVER("Rediscover") { ... } File: src/main/java/com/mindarray/job/JobScheduler.java
In `JobScheduler.REDISCOVER()`: Jobs for rediscovering existing objects to update their configuration
for triggering a rediscovery of all active network devices.

Rediscovery involves re-sending discovery packets to detect any changes in the network topology or device statuses. It helps in identifying newly added devices, removed devices, or changes in existing devices.

The job runs periodically with a fixed rate defined by DiscoveryEngineConfigConstants.DISCOVERY_ENGINE_REDISCOVER_INTERVAL. It ensures that the network inventory remains up-to-date and accurate.

This job should be scheduled carefully to avoid overwhelming the network with too many discovery requests at once. It typically runs less frequently than other discovery jobs to balance between accuracy and performance.

When this job is executed, it triggers a new task that: - Checks if there are any active devices to rediscover - Sends discovery packets to each active device - Updates the discovery status in the system

This job is critical for maintaining accurate network mapping and ensuring that the system has the latest information about the network devices. It helps in proactive monitoring and troubleshooting by providing up-to-date device data. @param context The JobExecutionContext containing job configuration and runtime data @return A future indicating the completion of the job execution @throws Exception If any error occurs during job execution */ public Future run(JobExecutionContext context) throws Exception { var logger = Logger.getLogger(JobScheduler.REDISCOVER().name()); return vertx.executeBlocking(promise -> { try { logger.info(String.format("starting %s...", JobScheduler.REDISCOVER())); var jobConfig = JsonObject.mapFrom(context.getJob()); jobConfig.put(JobEngineConstants.JOB_NAME, JobScheduler.REDISCOVER()); var rediscoverInterval = discoveryEngineConfigService.getDiscoveryEngineConfig().getJsonObject(DiscoveryEngineConfigConstants.DISCOVERY_ENGINE_REDISCOVER_INTERVAL).getLong(JobScheduler.REDISCOVER().name(), 1800000L); var delay = Math.max(jobConfig.getLong(JobEngineConstants.JOB_DELAY, 0), 60000L); if (System.currentTimeMillis() >= jobConfig.getLong(JobEngineConstants.LAST_EXECUTION_TIME, 0) + rediscoverInterval || delay > 0) { logger.info(String.format("executing %s with interval %s...", JobScheduler.REDISCOVER(), rediscoverInterval)); var discoverTask = new DiscoverTask(); discoverTask.setEventId(context.getEventId()); discoverTask.setContext(context); discoverTask.setJobConfig(jobConfig); discoverTask.setDelay(delay); vertx.eventBus().send(JobEngineConstants.JOB_SCHEDULER_ADDRESS, JsonObject.mapFrom(discoverTask)); } promise.complete(); } catch (Exception exception) { promise.fail(exception); } }); } /** * Returns the name of the Rediscover job. * * @return The name "Rediscover" as a string */ @Override public String name() { return "Rediscover"; How does ReportingEngine.process() work? Code signature: public process(Message message) { ... } File: src/main/java/com/mindarray/report/ReportingEngine.java
In `ReportingEngine.process()`: MOTADATA-1454, date format is shown as per default admin user and not by format selected by specific user. If report request is received from UI, fetch the user details from id and passed for headless authentication -- Changed method to update report progress on UI. Before, It is not showing 40%, 50% and directly getting exporting.. @param message Message
nts from the event bus.

This method is called whenever a new reporting event is received. It checks if the event is for a scheduled report, retrieves the necessary data, and sends it to the appropriate handler based on the report type (e.g., widget report, metric report). If the event is not for a scheduled report, it processes the report data directly.

The method also handles retries for failed report processing, ensuring that reports are generated even if temporary issues occur.

@param message The event message containing the reporting event data. The message should be in the following format: 

      {        "report.id": 12345,        "report.type": "WIDGET",        "report.name": "My Report",        "report.data": {          // Report data specific to the report type          // ...        },        "schedule": true, // Indicates if this is a scheduled report or not        "retry": 0        // Retry count for failed report processing (optional)      }
